Motivation: 多变量时间序列中存在四种结构不同的异常类型（点异常、分布异常、时序异常、集体异常），每种需要不同的特征表示，而现有无监督方法大多只针对其中一两种类型，且可解释性有限。

Method: 生成K=500个随机解析小波特征（跨越Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet四个小波族），每种针对特定异常类型；构建五个结构化隔离森林（每类型一个+一个元IF处理复合异常）；使用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值在0.1%-69.2%异常率范围内自动校准检测。

Result: 在mTSBench全部19个数据集上平均F1=0.228（13个可检测数据集F1=0.322），VUS-PR指标排名第一（0.463，较此前最佳0.329提升40.7%）。诊断框架识别出6个数据集对任何无监督方法基本不可检测。

Conclusion: 自适应阈值化（+38% F1）、四分支结构（+20%）和元IF（+23%）均被消融实验证明至关重要。CRAFTIIF通过构造性归因实现直接异常类型解释，无需事后解释。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [113] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出A2D2框架，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，在理论上保证收敛到奖励倾斜序列分布，实验上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调问题尚未被探索，现有方法局限于固定长度场景。

Method: 推导了联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，建立去掩码和插入质量作为最小化解码误差的可处理方法，提出自适应联合解码（AJD）损失函数，联合优化插入策略、去掩码策略和推理调度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性上优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调提供了一个统一且理论上可保证的框架。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [114] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种调整杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），通过硬编码杯积（cup product）和高阶规范理论的调整项，实现了规范不变的读出机制。理论证明在闭链上，输出完全取决于调整系数，该系数是规范不变信号的唯一来源，且对应的可观测量是一个非零二次型，在一阶和二阶规范变换下严格不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学和几何学中许多重要的可观测量都是上链的杯积（cup products），但传统方法缺乏固有的规范不变性。本文旨在设计一种神经层，通过引入高阶规范理论的调整项，使得读出结果天然具备规范不变性，从而更有效地提取物理/几何中的关键信息。

Method: 提出调整杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），将杯积与来自高阶规范理论的调整项硬编码为一个神经原语（neural primitive），从而在架构层面保证读出是规范不变的。

Result: 理论结果表明，在闭链（closed cycle）上，输出完全由调整系数决定；若将该系数设为零，则输出完全消失（无论其他参数如何）。调整系数是规范不变信号的唯一来源，且该可观测量是一个非零二次型，在一阶和二阶规范变换下严格不变。

Conclusion: 调整杯积神经层能够通过设计实现规范不变的读出，调整系数是产生规范不变信号的关键。这一方法为在神经网络中嵌入物理/几何对称性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [115] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重新定义为未来可观测性推断与数值估计的联合问题，通过双流架构（观测流和数值流）及三个专用模块建模观测动态与状态演化的交互。在包含自然缺失数据和工业数据的Shadow基准上，结合新指标OVJE，实验表明该方法显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不完整时间序列预测方法（如Neural ODEs、连续时间图网络）在推理时隐含假设未来有效观测的时间戳已知，但在实际系统中，未来是否会有有效观测本身是不确定的。因此需要同时预测未来观测的可观测性和数值。

Method: 提出Timeflies框架：1）双流架构（观测流+数值流）；2）可靠性感知嵌入模块；3）观测引导的依赖建模模块；4）联合预测模块；5）构建Shadow基准和OVJE评估指标。

Result: 大量实验表明Timeflies在多个数据集上持续优于现有方法，验证了显式建模未来可观测性的重要性。

Conclusion: 在不完整时间序列预测中，联合建模未来可观测性和数值估计比仅关注数值预测更有效，未来可观测性是一个被忽视但至关重要的维度。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [116] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“可模拟过程”框架，证明只要学习者能访问近似数据生成过程的模拟器，即使数据高度依赖，也能恢复与独立同分布数据相同的VC维学习保证，并展示了条件采样的统计与计算优势。最终给出一个统一算法，能在所有多项式时间可采样过程中学习任意VC类，遗憾受过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制，从而极大拓展了经典PAC模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论中大多数泛化结果依赖于数据独立性假设，而强依赖数据场景下的理论结果十分有限。为弥补这一空白，本文探索在最小假设下（即仅需一个可模拟数据生成过程的模拟器）能否恢复独立数据下的学习保证。

Method: 引入“可模拟过程”（simulatable processes）框架，学习者可访问一个近似数据生成分布的模拟器。利用该模拟器，通过条件采样等技术，推导出与经典独立数据设定相同的VC维依赖误差界。并进一步设计单一算法，该算法在多项式时间有界可采样过程上同时学习任意VC类，遗憾由时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Result: 1) 在可模拟过程框架下，恢复了与独立数据相同的VC维依赖学习保证；2) 条件采样在此设定下具有严格的统计与计算优势；3) 提出单一算法，可在所有多项式时间有界可采样过程上同时学习任意VC类。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓宽了经典PAC学习模型，证明即使在高度依赖的数据过程中，只要拥有合适的模拟器，仍能获得与独立数据相当的学习保证，为强依赖数据场景下的学习理论提供了新方向。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [117] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCSB提出了一种基于单纯形约束的稀疏装袋框架，通过优化OOB损失并引入凹二次惩罚解决L1-单纯形悖论，实现对装袋集成的后训练压缩与概率校准，最高可压缩96%的集成规模，同时提升校准质量和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准装袋集成（如随机森林、Bagged SVM/神经网络）对所有基估计器赋予均匀投票权重，忽略了各基估计器在不同局部区域的差异化能力，导致模型过度自信。现有方法缺乏对集成剪枝与概率校准的联合优化，且L1正则化在概率单纯形上因范数恒定而失效。

Method: 将集成剪枝与校准建模为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，最小化袋外（OOB）损失；为解决L1-单纯形悖论（L1范数在单纯形上为常数，无法诱导稀疏性），引入凹二次惩罚项以实现稀疏选择。该方法为模型无关的后训练框架。

Result: 最高实现96%的集成压缩，推理速度线性提升；校准误差（Expected Calibration Error）显著降低，泛化精度保持或提升。

Conclusion: SCSB是一种理论严谨、模型无关的装袋集成后训练压缩与校准框架，有效解决了均匀投票的过自信问题，并在压缩率、校准质量和泛化能力上均取得优异表现。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [118] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了链式思维推理中因果步骤的重要性，发现推理过程存在一个"承诺边界"——从暂态中间猜测到稳定高置信度答案的急剧转变，该边界通常出现在推理块结束前的单个步骤中，后续步骤对最终答案概率无影响。利用注意力探针可从中间步骤线性解码答案形成阶段，并以此实现早期退出，平均缩短CoT长度达55%且性能损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理是语言模型推理时扩展的主流范式，但其中单个步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不清楚，理解答案形成机制有助于优化推理效率。

Method: 通过早期退出估计每个步骤的因果重要性，识别承诺边界；使用注意力探针从中间推理步骤线性解码答案形成阶段；并利用该信号在承诺边界处提前退出推理块。

Result: 推理中存在从暂态猜测到稳定答案的承诺边界，该边界常在推理块结束前的单个步骤中发生；后续步骤为附随现象，不改变答案概率；注意力探针可高精度解码答案形成阶段并泛化到未见任务；早期退出可平均缩短CoT长度达55%且性能几乎无损。

Conclusion: 链式思维推理中的答案形成具有明确的因果边界，后续许多步骤是附随的；利用这一发现可大幅压缩推理长度而不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [119] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入恢复子空间维度(k_t)指标，发现灾难性遗忘中遗忘的知识仍保持紧凑可解码性，支持稳定恢复流形假说，表明遗忘本质是可达性与流形对齐问题而非信息破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战灾难性遗忘的传统认知（即先前知识被破坏），基于Accessibility Collapse框架探索持续学习中可恢复性的几何结构。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上使用顺序训练的ResNet-18，引入恢复子空间维度(k_t)指标，分析可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂度，并进行主角度漂移与可恢复性的相关性分析。

Result: 恢复维度保持稳定(平均k_t=8.0)；主角度漂移与可恢复性强相关(r=-0.862)；简单几何模型解释了82.2%的可恢复性方差。

Conclusion: 支持稳定恢复流形假说，表明灾难性遗忘本质是可达性和流形对齐问题而非信息破坏。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [120] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的更新特性，发现其更新具有稀疏性（坐标稀疏、FFN主导、跨层分布）和几何特性（数值满秩但谱集中、远离源权重的奇异子空间、落在源权重接近零的坐标上），表明密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写，而是保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）结合了在线策略学生轨迹和密集教师监督两种理想特性，已成为重要的后训练方法，但其如何改变模型参数的内在机制尚不清晰，本文旨在从稀疏性和几何特性两个维度揭示OPD的参数更新模式。

Method: 在多个语言和视觉-语言模型对上，从稀疏性（更新幅度、坐标稀疏程度、层间分布、FFN占比）和几何特性（数值秩、谱集中程度、与源权重的奇异子空间关系、权重幅值与更新幅值的关联）两个维度对OPD更新进行系统分析。

Result: （1）OPD更新稀疏：幅度小、坐标稀疏、跨层分布且以FFN为主，仅训练发现的子网络可恢复大部分OPD性能；但稀疏优化器SGD不如AdamW，因密集教师监督保留了异构梯度尺度。（2）OPD更新数值满秩但谱集中，大部分更新远离源权重的奇异子空间，且不成比例地落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写，OPD保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征，这一发现对理解蒸馏机制和优化后训练策略具有指导意义。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [121] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络中谱位置编码和游走位置编码在截断情况下的表达能力差异，发现截断后多种位置编码的表达能力发生根本性变化，且截断谱位置编码不再强于1-WL测试，实验表明混合使用截断位置编码效果更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常用的截断位置编码（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵的幂）的理论性质尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 理论分析截断后多种位置编码的表达能力差异，研究k-谐波距离等谱位置编码家族，并在真实数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 截断后多种位置编码的表达能力发生根本性变化；截断谱位置编码不再强于1-WL测试；不同截断位置编码之间存在表达能力差异；混合使用截断位置编码效果优于单一家族。

Conclusion: 截断操作会显著改变位置编码的理论性质，实践中混合使用不同截断位置编码是更优策略。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [ITME: Inference Tiered Memory Expansion with Disaggregated CXL-Hybrid Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12556)
*Hakbeom Jang,Younghoon Min,Sunwoong Kim,Taeyoung Ahn,Hanyee Kim,Youngpyo Joo,Hoshik Kim,Jongryool Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ITME（推理分层内存扩展）架构，利用CXL混合内存实现TB级字节可寻址远程内存扩展，用于支撑LLM中长上下文和智能体工作负载的共享上下文状态管理。通过确定性访问模式驱动数据在内存-存储层级间的主动迁移，在SK Hynix CMM和PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSD上验证性能，FPGA原型验证可行性，相比传统CPU卸载实现最高35.7%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM向智能体和长上下文工作负载的快速转变，使得单个服务器无法处理TB级上下文状态，行业需要向解耦的共享存储架构演进。现有方案（如JBOF+DPU架构）虽加速了NVMe-oF目标处理，但仍需复杂的软件优化且成本效益不足，亟需一种更优的架构来扩展共享上下文基础设施。

Method: 提出ITME架构，核心是采用CXL混合内存提供TB级字节可寻址的远程内存扩展。关键洞察是模型权重和前缀缓存的确定性访问模式，使得系统能够主动管理数据在内存-存储层级间的移动。利用生产级SK Hynix CMM和PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSD进行性能评估，并通过FPGA硬件原型验证功能可行性。

Result: ITME通过提供额外的远程内存扩展来容纳超出主机内存限制的大KV缓存足迹，相比传统CPU卸载实现了最高35.7%的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: ITME通过CXL混合内存实现了成本高效的可扩展共享上下文基础设施，简化了软件栈（直接字节可寻址），有效解决了共享上下文基础设施面临的挑战，为LLM推理的规模化部署提供了有前景的方案。

Abstract: The rapid shift toward agentic and long-context workloads in Large Language Models (LLMs) is pushing the industry beyond the capacity of individual servers toward disaggregated shared storage to handle TB-scale context states. This movement has led to the emergence of specialized shared context layers designed to externalize and share cumulative inference states across distributed clusters. While offloading to a data processing unit (DPU) within just-a-bunch-of-flash (JBOF) architectures accelerates NVMe-over-fabrics (NVMe-oF) target processing, the need for sophisticated software-level optimization and cost-efficiency burdens remain significant. Consequently, the ideal architecture for scaling this shared context infrastructure is still an active area of exploration. In this paper, we propose ITME (Inference Tiered Memory Expansion), which leverages a CXL-hybrid memory to present a massive, TB-scale byte-addressable remote memory expansion. This approach enables cost-efficient scaling and simplifies the software stack through direct byte-addressability, effectively addressing the challenges of shared context infrastructure. Our key insight is that the deterministic access patterns of voluminous model weights and prefix caches enable the system to proactively manage data movement across the memory-storage hierarchy. We validate ITME by evaluating its performance potential with production-grade SK Hynix CMM and PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSDs, while further demonstrating its functional feasibility through an FPGA-based hardware prototype. Overall, ITME enhances conventional CPU-offloading by providing additional remote memory expansion to accommodate large KV cache footprints beyond host memory limits, achieving up to a 35.7\% throughput improvement.

</details>


### [123] [Eidola: Modeling Multi-GPU Network Communication Traffic in Distributed AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12638)
*Ranganath R. Selagamsetty,Matthew Poremba,Bradford M. Beckmann,Joshua San Miguel,Mikko H. Lipasti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Eidola，一个基于gem5的可扩展仿真框架扩展，用于建模多GPU系统间的通信流量。Eidola通过注释时序配置文件模拟点对点GPU写入，支持可配置的流量模式和同步行为分析，在融合内核执行和同步机制研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式AI工作负载的扩展使得多GPU系统成为训练大模型的必需品。内核融合和计算通信重叠等技术虽然减少了延迟，但引入了不规则的瞬态流量模式，现有工具难以建模。这些技术依赖细粒度同步和点对点通信，对互连带宽和延迟造成了巨大压力。

Method: Eidola是gem5仿真框架的可扩展扩展。其GPU模型作为简洁的"eidolon（幻象）"，仅模拟流量建模所需的最小特征。利用真实应用的注释时序配置文件，以周期级精度模拟点对点GPU写入，支持可配置的每GPU流量模式和隔离性能分析。

Result: 通过复现融合内核执行的可变性以及实现SyncMon启发的同步机制，证实了轮询相关内存流量的减少。结果表明Eidola为研究多GPU通信和架构探索提供了灵活可扩展的平台。

Conclusion: Eidola是一个灵活且可扩展的平台，能够支持对现代分布式GPU系统中互连通信的详细研究和架构探索，有效解决了现有工具难以建模不规则瞬态流量的问题。

Abstract: As distributed AI workloads grow in scale, multi-GPU systems have become essential for training large models. Although techniques like kernel fusion and overlapping communication with computation help reduce delays, they also introduce irregular and transient traffic patterns that are difficult to model using existing tools. These techniques rely heavily on fine-grained synchronization and peer-to-peer communication, which place significant pressure on interconnect bandwidth and latency.
  In this work, we introduce Eidola, a scalable extension to the gem5 simulation framework that enables detailed modeling of inter-GPU communication traffic. The extension is scalable as our GPU model serves as a succinct eidolon, emulating the minimal characteristics needed for traffic modeling. Eidola uses annotated timing profiles from real applications to emulate peer-to-peer GPU writes with cycle-level precision. This allows researchers to simulate and analyze synchronization behavior across large multi-GPU configurations. The simulator supports configurable per-GPU traffic patterns and enables isolated performance analysis under different communication scenarios.
  We demonstrate Eidola's effectiveness by reproducing variability in fused kernel execution and by implementing a SyncMon-inspired synchronization mechanism, confirming reductions in polling-related memory traffic. Our results show that Eidola provides a flexible and scalable platform for studying inter-GPU communication and supports architectural exploration in modern distributed GPU systems.

</details>


### [124] [On the Limits of Performance Portability in Directive-Based GPU Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12753)
*Alessandro Romeo,Nitin Shukla,Stefano Truzzi,Alessio Suriano,Andrea Mignone*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于指令的GPU编程（OpenACC vs OpenMP）在NVIDIA A100和AMD MI250X上的性能可移植性，发现OpenMP在AMD平台上的应用级速度比NVIDIA的OpenACC基线慢约3倍，内核级慢最多达10倍，低并行度内核因C++抽象层导致寄存器溢出，最高慢47倍。结果表明跨架构可移植性能不仅需要应用层改动，还需编译器后端和架构感知优化策略的持续进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用向GPU加速的百亿亿次系统迁移受到性能、可移植性和生产力之间权衡的制约，亟需评估指令式GPU编程框架在实际生产级代码中的性能可移植性。

Method: 将生产级磁流体动力学代码gPLUTO从OpenACC移植到OpenMP，并在NVIDIA A100（Leonardo Booster）和AMD MI250X（LUMI-G）设备上进行性能分析，通过内核级分析和编译器后端对比来量化差异。

Result: 在NVIDIA平台上OpenACC与OpenMP性能相当；在AMD MI250X上OpenMP应用级速度约为NVIDIA A100 OpenACC基线的1/3，内核级慢速最高达10倍；低并行度内核中C++抽象层导致寄存器溢出，极端情况慢47倍；运行时主导因素为内存延迟瓶颈而非峰值带宽限制。

Conclusion: 实现跨GPU架构的可移植性能需要应用层代码调整、编译器后端改进以及架构感知优化策略的共同推进。

Abstract: The transition of scientific applications to GPU-accelerated exascale systems is constrained by trade-offs between performance, portability, and productivity. This work evaluates the performance portability of directive-based GPU programming by porting gPLUTO, a production-grade magnetohydrodynamics code for astrophysical simulations, from OpenACC to OpenMP, and analyzing its performance on NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) and AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) devices. On NVIDIA platforms, OpenACC and OpenMP achieve comparable performance due to a shared compiler backend, providing a consistent baseline for assessing algorithmic efficiency. In contrast, the same OpenMP implementation is approximately three times slower at the application level on AMD MI250X with respect to the NVIDIA A100 OpenACC baseline, with kernel-level slowdowns reaching up to an order of magnitude, driven by sensitivity to strided memory-access patterns and compiler limitations. Kernel-level profiling shows that the dominant contributors to run-time are memory-latency-bound rather than limited by peak band-width. In low-parallelism kernels, C++ abstraction layers increase register pressure and spilling, leading to extreme slowdowns of up to 47x in specific cases. These results indicate that portable performance across GPU architectures requires not only application-level changes but also continued advances in compiler backends and architecture-aware optimization strategies

</details>


### [125] [SemanticXR: Low Power and Real-time Queryable Semantic Mapping with an Object-Level Device-Cloud Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12849)
*Rahul Singh,Devdeep Ray,Connor Smith,Sarita Adve*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SemanticXR 是首个面向扩展现实（XR）设备的端-云协同语义建图与查询系统，通过以对象为第一级通信、执行和存储单元，在功耗、带宽和内存受限条件下实现实时、开放词汇的语义建图，相比基线方案在服务器端建图延迟提升2.2倍，上行带宽低于2.5 Mbps，设备端查询延迟低于100ms，额外功耗仅2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义建图方案计算密集、依赖服务器级资源，无法直接部署在移动XR设备上；而云端卸载方案缺乏跨设备-云边界的语义建图拆分设计，未对通信、执行和内存开销进行系统管理。

Method: 提出以语义可识别对象作为通信、执行和存储的一级单元。服务端采用对象级并行与几何下采样降低建图延迟，对象级深度-建图协同设计降低上行带宽；设备端构建对象级稀疏局部地图，通过增量更新与更新优先级实现网络鲁棒查询，并支持可配置的资源-质量权衡。

Result: 服务器端建图延迟提升2.2倍（同等语义质量）；上行带宽低于2.5 Mbps；设备端查询延迟低于100ms（支持多达10,000个对象，含网络中断场景）；500 MB内存可支持数万对象；下行带宽随地图变化扩展；正常运行额外功耗仅2%。

Conclusion: SemanticXR 验证了以对象为中心的设备-云协同架构在XR场景下的有效性，实现了实时、开放词汇、低功耗的语义建图与查询，为资源受限的移动XR设备提供了可行的部署方案。

Abstract: Semantic mapping is a core service that enables grounded interactions in emerging Extended Reality (XR) applications such as AI assistants and spatial object search. Deploying this capability on mobile XR devices requires a system that is open-vocabulary, real-time, and low-power. Existing approaches are compute-intensive and assume server-class resources. Cloud offloading offers a practical path, but no existing system splits semantic mapping across the device-cloud boundary or manages its communication, execution, and memory footprint.
  We present SemanticXR, the first device-cloud system for real-time, open-vocabulary semantic mapping and querying under XR power, bandwidth, and memory constraints. Our key insight is to elevate semantically identifiable objects to first-class units of communication, execution, and memory across the device and server. On the server, object-level parallelism and geometry downsampling improve mapping latency, while object-level depth-mapping co-design reduces upstream bandwidth. On the device, an object-level sparse local map with incremental updates and update prioritization enables network-robust querying with bounded memory and downstream bandwidth. Object-level configurable resource usage vs. quality trade-offs let applications and the system adapt mapping to application requirements and operating conditions, respectively.
  Against a device-cloud baseline with the same perception models, object-level organization improves server-side mapping latency by 2.2X at equal semantic quality. Depth-mapping co-design maintains upstream bandwidth under 2.5 Mbps. On the device, SemanticXR sustains sub-100 ms query latency for up to 10,000 objects even under network drops, supports tens of thousands of objects within 500 MB, and scales downstream bandwidth with map changes, not total scene size. The system adds only 2% device power during normal operation.

</details>


### [126] [High-Order Spectral Element Methods for Wave Propagation on ARM Multicore CPU with SME: Optimizations and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12850)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Wubing Wan,Zekun Yin,Wenqiang Wang,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于谱元法（SEM）的地震波传播HPC负载，在支持可扩展矩阵扩展（SME）的ARM多核处理器上，提出了一种SME感知的优化方案。通过SME感知的小矩阵批处理内核、面向有限HBM系统的混合MPI+OpenMP执行方案以及基于色散的等精度分析，在LX2处理器上实现了4-6倍的整体性能提升，并发现SME改变了SEM在色散等精度前沿上更优的工作点，向更高多项式阶偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的谱元法（SEM）实现在支持可扩展矩阵扩展（SME）的新型ARM多核CPU（如LX2处理器）上未能充分利用硬件特性，存在性能匹配不佳的问题。

Method: 1) 设计了SME感知的批处理小矩阵内核，用于SEM张量积算子；2) 提出了面向有限HBM系统的内存感知混合MPI+OpenMP执行方案；3) 开展了基于色散的等精度（iso-accuracy）研究，分析(h,p)权衡。

Result: 在固定多项式阶数下，优化后的实现相比原始代码性能提升4-6倍，并优于优化后的非SME CPU基线；同时SME使性能有利的工作点沿色散等精度前沿向更高多项式阶偏移，减少了求解时间和工作集大小。

Conclusion: SME不仅影响内核效率，还改变了SEM在现代ARM多核平台上的实际离散化权衡。

Abstract: Wave propagation based on the spectral element method (SEM) is a representative HPC workload, but existing SEM implementations are not well matched to emerging ARM multicore CPUs with Scalable Matrix Extension (SME). We present an SME-enabled optimization of \textsc{SPECFEM3D} on the emerging LX2 processor that combines an SME-aware batched small-matrix kernel for SEM tensor-product operators, a memory-aware hybrid MPI+OpenMP execution scheme for limited-HBM systems, and a dispersion-based iso-accuracy study of the $(h,p)$ tradeoff. At fixed polynomial order, the optimized implementation improves full-application performance by 4--6$\times$ over the original code and delivers clear gains over optimized non-SME CPU baselines. Beyond these implementation-level gains, our results suggest that SME shifts the performance-favorable operating point toward higher polynomial orders along the dispersion-based iso-accuracy frontier, further reducing time-to-solution and working-set size. These results indicate that SME affects not only kernel efficiency, but also the practical discretization tradeoff for SEM on modern ARM multicore platforms.

</details>


### [127] [Maestro: Workload-Aware Cross-Cluster Scheduling for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12950)
*Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Yihui Zhang,Yilong Li,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Maestro是一个面向LLM多智能体系统的工作负载感知调度系统，通过利用智能体语义预测输出长度和内存使用，在节点、集群和全局三个层面进行分层调度，在严格GPU预算下显著提升资源效率和SLO达标率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-MAS部署面临三大挑战：解码阶段成本非线性且输入依赖、多模型需求导致内存碎片化和过度配置、跨集群调度存在权衡，亟需面向多智能体工作流的专用调度系统。

Method: Maestro利用智能体语义和角色预测每阶段输出长度和内存使用，驱动分层调度器：节点层通过层次化权重缓存和弹性内存配置实现动态多模型共存；集群层执行延迟感知路由避免冷启动和内存过载；全局层实施工作流感知优先级调度减少头端阻塞。

Result: KV保留HBM减少67.2%，高争用场景下SLO达标率比EDF提升23.6个百分点。

Conclusion: Maestro通过显式利用智能体语义和角色信息进行工作负载感知调度，有效解决了LLM-MAS在资源受限云环境中的部署挑战，验证了利用多智能体系统特殊语义信息优化调度性能的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Model based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for tackling complex tasks by breaking them into collaborative workflows of specialized LLM-powered agents. However, deploying such multi-agent workloads at scale poses significant system challenges. Each user query spawns an iterative pipeline of LLM calls, greatly amplifying resource consumption compared to single-turn queries. In resource-constrained cloud settings, these workflows face non-deterministic and input-dependent costs at decode stage, heavy-tailed multi-model requirements with memory fragmentation and over-provisioning, and cross-cluster scheduling trade-offs. We present Maestro, a workload-aware scheduling system designed for LLM-MAS serving under strict GPU budgets. Maestro explicitly leverages agent semantics and roles: it predicts the output length and memory usage of each stage and uses this prediction to drive a hierarchical scheduler. At the node level, Maestro enables dynamic multi-model co-location via hierarchical weight caching and elastic memory provisioning. At the cluster level, it performs latency-aware routing to avoid cold-start delays and memory overloads. At the global level, it enforces workflow-aware prioritization to minimize head-of-line blocking for interactive tasks. Across prototype experiments and trace-driven simulations, Maestro reduces KV-reservation HBM by 67.2% and improves high-contention SLO attainment over EDF by 23.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [128] [JiRAIYA: A Reputation-Based Hierarchical Federated Learning Framework on Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13180)
*Venkata Raghava Kurada,Pallav Kumar Baruah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Web3技术的联邦学习框架，采用分层架构和委托管理者机制，通过异常检测、共识机制和基于信誉分数的备份机制，实现了透明、可信且可审计的联邦学习过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统在企业环境中缺乏透明性和可审计性，聚合过程不透明，且现有的缓解策略依赖外部验证者，带来额外的计算和通信开销。

Method: 采用分层架构，委托管理者协调各自联邦内的FL训练过程；结合异常检测与共识机制抵御对抗和投毒攻击；模型更新被编码广播给所有管理者独立评估，通过共识的更新才纳入全局模型；采用基于信誉分数的备份机制确保模型生成。

Result: 在真实场景下的广泛实验验证了该框架的有效性和鲁棒性，展示了其在传统企业环境之外实现透明联邦学习的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过Web3技术显著提升了联邦学习的透明度、信任度和可审计性，为超越传统企业场景的联邦学习部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning(FL) is predominantly deployed in enterprise environments, where limited transparency and restricted auditability hinder broader adoption. Existing FL systems often suffer from opaque aggregation processes, making it unclear which model updates are accepted or discarded. Current mitigation strategies typically rely on external validators introducing additional computational and communication overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel FL framework that leverages existing Web3 technologies to enhance transparency, trust and auditability throughout the training process. The framework adopts a hierarchical architecture in which delegated managers orchestrate the FL training process within their respective federations. To mitigate adversarial and poisoning attacks, a combination of novelty detection and consensus mechanisms were employed. Model updates are encoded and broad casted to all managers, who independently evaluate their validity and those model updates that are approved by the consensus are incorporated into the global model. Additionally, a reputation score based backup mechanism is employed to ensure model generation. Extensive experiments conducted under real world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness, resilience of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential to enable transparent FL beyond traditional enterprise setting.

</details>


### [129] [Dynamic Resource Management in Production HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13266)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Guillaume Houzeaux,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的MPI malleability方法，通过DMR框架实现，无需修改应用代码或调度器即可在三个TOP500超算上实现动态资源管理，在保持性能的同时大幅减少节点小时消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学应用呈现时变行为，但生产HPC集群仍依赖固定尺寸分配，现有动态技术大多停留在实验室原型，亟需一种能与现有软件栈和运维实践兼容的实用可塑性方案。

Method: 提出DMR框架，通过非侵入式API集成到现有科学应用中，无需修改调度器或进行侵入式代码变更，在三个TOP500超算上使用原生资源管理器进行部署验证。

Result: 在受控环境中性能与静态基线相当，同时显著降低了相同工作负载的节点小时消耗。成功在三个TOP500超算上部署了两个大规模科学应用。

Conclusion: 可塑性可以在使用原生资源管理器的生产系统上有效利用，降低了HPC中动态资源管理的采用门槛。

Abstract: Many large-scale scientific applications exhibit time-varying behavior, yet production HPC clusters still rely on rigid, fixed-size allocations, and most dynamic techniques remain confined to laboratory prototypes. This work presents a practical MPI malleability methodology that integrates with state-of-the-art high-performance computing (HPC) software stacks and operational practices. The methodology is implemented in the Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) framework and is designed to ease adoption by existing applications without requiring intrusive code changes or scheduler modifications. We evaluate our approach by integrating the DMR API into two large-scale scientific applications and deploying them on three TOP500 supercomputers under realistic production configurations. Our non-invasive malleability solution achieves performance comparable to static baselines in controlled environments while substantially reducing node-hour consumption for identical workloads. These results show that malleability can be effectively exploited on production systems using vanilla resource managers, lowering the barrier to adoption of dynamic resource management in HPC.

</details>


### [130] [Skiplists with Foresight: Skipping Cache Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13321)
*Tomer Cory,Niv Sulimany,Erez Petrank*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Foresight，一种面向缓存友好的跳表优化技术，在顺序和并发场景下均可提升性能，微基准测试中吞吐量提升最高达45%，在DBx1000内存数据库中端到端性能提升最高达15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳表是系统和应用中广泛使用的核心数据结构，但现有设计在缓存利用方面存在不足，亟需一种缓存友好的优化方案来提升其性能。

Method: 提出Foresight优化技术，识别并解决并发环境下的同步挑战，将其集成到1个顺序跳表和3个并发跳表设计中。

Result: 微基准测试中吞吐量提升最高达45%；在DBx1000内存数据库中端到端性能提升最高达15%。

Conclusion: Foresight是一种精确的、易于集成到多种跳表设计中的优化方案，能有效提升跳表在不同场景下的性能表现。

Abstract: A skiplist is a fundamental data structure widely used in systems and applications for indexing data stores. In this work, we introduce Foresight, a cache-friendly skiplist optimization. Extending Foresight to concurrent settings introduces significant synchronization challenges that we identify and address. Foresight is a surgical optimization, easy to integrate into a wide variety of skiplist designs. We apply it to one sequential and three concurrent skiplist designs and observe throughput improvements of up to 45% in microbenchmarks. When applied to a skiplist-based index in the DBx1000 in-memory database, Foresight yields end-to-end performance gains of up to 15%.

</details>


### [131] [Work Stealing for the 2D-Mesh Topology of Satellite Constellations in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13329)
*Mia Reitz,Dorian Chenet,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向空间边缘计算的邻域专属工作窃取策略，通过限制仅在直接相连的邻居之间进行任务窃取，避免了多跳通信延迟。实验表明，该策略在性能上与传统全局窃取策略相当，且理论模型显示其在规模扩大时更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步多任务运行时系统假设全连接网络和低延迟，采用全局工作窃取策略。然而，空间边缘计算中的低轨卫星星座形成稀疏网状拓扑，多跳通信带来显著延迟，现有AMT方法无法直接适用，因此需要针对SEC环境调整工作窃取策略。

Method: 提出邻域专属工作窃取策略（neighbor-only work stealing），即工作者仅从直接相连的邻居节点窃取任务，避免多跳通信。同时建立了分析模型计算延迟优势，并在HPC集群上通过模拟网状拓扑进行实验验证。

Result: 分析模型表明，邻域专属策略的每次窃取尝试延迟优势随星座规模增大而增长。初步实验显示，在均匀低延迟链路上，邻域专属策略与全局窃取策略的性能差距在约2.2%以内，无论是均衡负载还是不规则负载。

Conclusion: 邻域专属工作窃取策略在负载均衡方面与全局窃取相当，而分析模型表明随着系统规模扩大，邻域专属策略更具优势，为将AMT适应到空间边缘计算提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) is a parallel programming model used in High Performance Computing (HPC). An AMT runtime can distribute fine-grained tasks across processing units called workers, through work stealing: when a worker has no tasks left to process, it tries to steal tasks from other workers. Workers are not restricted to a single compute node but can also be distributed across multiple nodes of an HPC cluster. Existing AMT runtimes assume a fully connected network with low, uniform latency and perform global work stealing, selecting another worker at random from all workers in the system.
  Space Edge Computing (SEC) uses constellations of satellites in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) as distributed compute clusters. Unlike HPC clusters, LEO satellites communicate through inter-satellite links that form a sparse mesh topology. Reaching a distant satellite requires multiple hops, each adding latency.
  As a step toward adapting AMT to SEC, this paper proposes a neighbor-only work stealing strategy in which workers steal exclusively from directly connected neighbors, avoiding multi-hop communication. An analytical model shows that restricting stealing this way yields a per-attempt latency advantage that grows with constellation size. Preliminary experiments on an HPC cluster with an emulated mesh over uniform low-latency links isolate the effect of victim selection: the neighbor-only strategy performs within ~2.2% of global stealing on both balanced and irregular workloads, indicating that restricting the victim set does not harm load balancing in this setting. Taken together, the experiments suggest that neighbor-only stealing can be on a par with global stealing, and the model suggests that neighbor-only stealing becomes preferable at scale.

</details>


### [132] [Temporal Conductance and Bounds on the Voter Model for Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13374)
*Tatiana Rocha Avila,Holger Dell,John Lapinskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文引入时序传导性(temporal conductance)Φ，一种适用于动态网络的连通性度量，并将Berenbrink等人关于投票模型在静态网络上共识时间的界O(m/(d_min φ))推广到动态网络，证明预期共识时间至多为O(m/(d_min Φ))，且该界在常数因子内是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态网络中，静态传导性在任一快照不连通时即降为0，无法刻画跨时间步的连通性。需要一种能够捕捉不同时间出现的边所构成的连通性的更一般的度量。

Method: 引入时序传导性(temporal conductance)Φ作为动态网络的连通性度量，将Berenbrink等人的分析框架从静态传导性推广到时序传导性，并证明上界的紧性。

Result: 标准投票模型在动态网络上的预期共识时间上界为O(m/(d_min Φ))，且该界在常数因子内是紧的。

Conclusion: 时序传导性可作为分析时序网络上其他动力学以及更一般的时间非齐次马尔可夫链的有用工具。

Abstract: The voter model is a classical stochastic process that models how opinions might spread through a network: at each step, every node lazily adopts the opinion of a random neighbour; eventually all nodes share the same opinion (consensus). Stronger connectivity should yield faster consensus. Berenbrink, Giakkoupis, Kermarrec, and Mallmann-Trenn (ICALP 2016) make this precise via the network's conductance: if the network has $m$ edges, minimum degree $d_{\min}$, and conductance at least $φ$, then the voter model reaches consensus in expected $O(m/(d_{\min}φ))$ steps. Their results extend to dynamic networks with fixed vertex degrees by considering the network's conductance at each time step.
  We introduce temporal conductance $Φ$, a more general connectivity measure for dynamic networks. Unlike static conductance, which collapses to $0$ whenever some snapshot is disconnected, $Φ$ captures connectivity through edges that appear at different times. We generalise the results of Berenbrink et al. from static conductance to temporal conductance, showing that the expected consensus time of the standard voter model is at most $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is tight up to constant factors. We expect temporal conductance to be a useful primitive for analysing other dynamics on temporal networks, and potentially time-inhomogeneous Markov chains more generally.

</details>


### [133] [GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13501)
*Xinwei Qiang,Yifan Hu,Shixuan Sun,Jing Yang,Han Zhao,Chen Chen,Yu Feng,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出GF-DiT，一个策略可编程的弹性DiT服务运行时系统，通过将GPU并行度作为可调度资源、引入异步执行抽象和无组集合通信机制，实现对扩散Transformer请求的动态并行度调整，相比静态并行执行，吞吐量提升最高6.01倍，平均延迟降低最高95%，SLO违规率降低最高90%，通信组建立开销从778ms降至约60μs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DiT服务系统为每个请求分配固定的并行配置，但DiT工作负载在请求、执行阶段和系统条件上存在显著异构性，导致静态并行度效率低下，GPU利用率差，服务质量下降。

Method: GF-DiT将GPU并行度作为可调度资源，提出异步执行抽象将请求分解为可独立调度的轨迹任务，实现在线GPU重分配；提出无组集合通信抽象，支持低开销的在线执行组形成和重配置。

Result: 在vLLM-Omni上实现并评估，相比静态并行流水线执行：吞吐量提升最高6.01倍，平均延迟降低最高95%，SLO违规率降低最高90%，通信组建立开销从778ms降至约60μs。

Conclusion: GF-DiT证明了弹性并行在DiT服务中的可行性和有效性，为异构工作负载下的扩散模型推理服务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality.
  This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups.
  We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.

</details>


### [134] [Finding Conservation Laws of Large Dynamical Systems with Tasks and Futures: A Case Study in Utilizing Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13623)
*Rüdiger Nather*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的future语义构造await_delete，允许值在被消费后安全重用，解决了future值不可变限制无法进行高效线性代数中原地内存更新的问题。基于此，提出了用于稠密对称矩阵分块求逆的future算法，并在扩展的Taskflow中实现。实验表明，future在小规模问题上开销较大，但在大规模矩阵上接近线性扩展，证明其在大规模线性代数中的高性能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统future模型要求值不可变，这限制了在高效线性代数中必须进行原地内存更新的能力，而内存回收对高性能至关重要。随着并行工作负载日益复杂，管理细粒度数据依赖成为关键挑战，现有future模型无法满足这一需求。

Method: 引入新构造await_delete，扩展传统future语义，允许值在消费者完成消费后被安全重用。基于此扩展，提出了一个面向稠密对称矩阵分块求逆的future算法，灵感来源于动力系统守恒律的求解算法。将该方法实现在扩展的Taskflow框架中。

Result: 强扩展实验表明，future在小规模问题上存在显著开销，但在大规模矩阵上实现了近乎线性的扩展。分析了摊销阈值，表明future是大规模线性代数的高性能可行工具。

Conclusion: 通过扩展future语义引入await_delete构造，解决了不可变限制下的内存重用问题，为大规模线性代数中future的高效应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As parallel workloads grow in complexity, managing fine-grained data dependencies becomes a critical challenge. Futures offer a promising model for handling these dependencies, particularly in irregular algorithms, but they also come with the restriction of value-immutability. This immutability limits the ability to perform in-place memory updates, a necessity for high-performance linear algebra where memory recycling is paramount.
  In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a new construct, await_delete, which extends traditional future semantics to allow safe value reuse once consumers are finished. Building on this extension, we present a novel future-based algorithm for the block-wise inversion of dense, symmetric matrices, motivated by a recent algorithm for finding conservation laws of dynamical systems.
  We implement our approach in an extended version of Taskflow and evaluate it through strong-scaling experiments. Our results demonstrate that while futures incur significant overhead on smaller problem sizes, they achieve nearly linear scaling on large matrices. We analyze the amortization threshold and show that futures are a viable high-performance tool for large-scale linear algebra.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense诊断框架，发现参数化工具检索模型在模糊查询下性能大幅下降（最多下降64个百分点），且存在知识-检索分离现象：模型即使检索表现良好，对工具事实的理解也接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化工具检索评估存在两个缺陷：1) 使用完整指定的详细查询，不符合真实场景；2) 采用约束解码限制输出为有效token路径，无法真正检验模型对工具的理解。因此需要更全面的诊断框架。

Method: 提出ToolSense开源诊断框架，自动从任意工具目录生成三类基准测试：1) 真实检索基准(RRB)，包含三个歧义级别的查询；2) MCQ探测基准；3) QA探测基准。在ToolBench (~47k工具)上评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 在RRB模糊查询上，多个参数化模型配置性能比完全指定查询的ToolBench基准下降约50-64个百分点，甚至低于嵌入模型基线。部分检索表现良好的模型在事实探测上得分接近随机，揭示了知识-检索分离现象。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索模型在模糊真实场景下表现脆弱，且检索能力与工具知识理解存在脱节，需要更好的评估方法和模型设计。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [136] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor 是一个多智能体框架，通过结构化树搜索作为认知层，在大规模有状态动作空间中进行自主优化，在LLM推理优化任务上实现了最高193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，远超单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主优化系统仅在隔离的目标上运行无状态评估，缺乏对大规模、有状态动作空间的有效探索能力；LLM推理优化需要跨应用、框架、编译器、内核和硬件栈的协调工程努力，缺乏自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出Arbor多智能体框架，核心包括：(1) 结构化树搜索作为认知层，维护显式的评分假设搜索树作为共享工作记忆；(2) Orchestrator智能体驱动优化并委派领域专家；(3) Critic智能体通过根因分析、内省和测量验证保障稳定性；(4) 将智能体能力分解为硬技能（领域专长）和软技能（协调协议），支持完全自主的多日优化活动。

Result: 在LLM推理优化任务上，Arbor实现高达193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，而单智能体仅达到+33%吞吐量改进并在数小时内不可恢复地崩溃；该方法在多个硬件代际上表现稳定，运行间方差在2个百分点以内。

Conclusion: Arbor通过多智能体协作与树搜索认知架构，有效解决了大规模有状态动作空间中的自主优化问题，具有硬件无关性、可复现性，且显著超越单智能体方法及供应商优化的基线。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [137] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AI智能体的战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用，同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误（即智能体独立行动但支持本可改善其输出的概率）。在群体层面，最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则；在线算法通过自适应阈值和随机探索控制错误率，并引入即时校准方法减少不必要的支持调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策支持研究人类如何利用机器学习模型做决策，但在现代智能体系统中角色反转：AI智能体代表用户行动，人类和工具成为支持机制。这种角色反转带来了可靠性问题，智能体错误后果严重，且行为必须与人类目标和约束保持一致。本文旨在重新审视决策支持的两个基本原则（支持的代价-价值权衡和不确定性量化的作用），以AI智能体为核心建立框架。

Method: 提出一个战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用，同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误（CFMSE）。群体层面证明最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则。开发在线算法，通过自适应阈值和随机探索控制CFMSE，无需分布假设。引入即时校准方法减少不必要的支持调用。

Result: 在信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景中实验表明，该方法能可靠地控制目标错误率，同时在实际中大幅减少支持使用。

Conclusion: 该框架将多种AI场景统一到战略决策支持视角下，为构建可靠、高效的智能体系统提供了理论基础和实践方法。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [138] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pythagoras-Prover，一个高效开源Lean定理证明器系列，包含4B和32B自回归模型及首个4B扩散模型。通过课程微调、动态证明筛选、增强形式化(ALF)等技术，Pythagoras-Prover-4B以167倍少参数量超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B，32B模型在MiniF2F-Test达到93.0%并解决93道Putnam题，均刷新开源SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Lean定理证明器依赖大量训练和推理计算，原因在于已验证证明数据稀缺且形式证明搜索的推理轨迹长，导致监督微调和采样成本高昂。本文旨在构建计算高效的开源证明器，降低对计算资源的依赖。

Method: 1) 构建按难度分层的Lean验证语料库(易/中/难)用于课程SFT；2) 动态证明推理过滤方案，将每个实例控制在8k token上下文；3) 提出增强型Lean形式化(ALF)，通过扰动已知问题生成变体并经自蒸馏扩充训练信号；4) 首个基于扩散模型的证明器(4B)，在推理时迭代精炼证明。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B在MiniF2F-Test上pass@32达86.1%，超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B的82.4%(参数少约167倍)；32B模型达93.0%，刷新开源SOTA，并在PutnamBench上解决93/672题。发布MiniF2F-ALF基准，所有模型在其上精度下降。

Conclusion: Pythagoras-Prover系列证明了在有限计算预算下训练高性能定理证明器的可行性，课程学习、动态过滤和ALF数据增强等策略有效提升了数据效率和模型性能，扩散模型在定理证明领域展现出初步潜力。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [139] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaDrive是一个三阶段流水线，利用风格指令下的人类驾驶演示作为上下文示例，条件化VLA驾驶代理，实现无需重新训练即可切换不同驾驶风格（激进/中立/保守），在Bench2Drive上驾驶评分提升4.6%-5.4%，并产生风格多样的非Ego交通代理用于闭环仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶仿真中的交通代理行为模式单一，要么基于规则，要么学习单一行为模式。风格变化方法依赖于事后标签或LLM推断的奖励权重，而非人类在明确风格指令下驾驶的真实演示，缺乏对真实人类风格驾驶行为的直接建模。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(i) 利用图文相似度对风格指令下的人类驾驶数据进行离线三元组挖掘；(ii) 训练轻量级检索头，融合冻结的视觉特征与小控制编码器，构建按风格组织的数据库；(iii) 微调单一VLA骨干网络，将检索到的上下文点作为行为演示进行waypoint预测。推理时通过切换检索数据库实现风格切换，无需重新训练。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上，无风格条件下驾驶评分比SimLingo高4.6%，比HiP-AD高2.5%；风格条件下每种风格均取得最高驾驶评分，最弱风格比最强基线DMW高5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度和加速度分别提升18%和25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive有效利用真实人类风格驾驶演示作为上下文示例，实现了无需逐风格重新训练即可切换驾驶风格的能力，同时生成了风格多样的人类化非Ego交通代理，提升了闭环仿真的真实性和多样性。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [140] ["Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12618)
*Alan Cooney,David Africa,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型中的谎言检测问题，发现现有训练模型有机体未能确保模型真正相信与表述相反的内容，导致检测结果难以解释。为此构建了13个推理模型有机体（信念在思维链中验证并泛化）和Varied Deception提示式撒谎测试床。评估了四种检测器：思维链判断器、logprob分类器、两种激活探针（含新方法DYL）。结果表明：提示撒谎场景下检测性能随模型能力正缩放，但激活和logprob检测器在训练有机体上表现急剧下降（DYL保留最强信号），仅思维链判断器保持0.82平衡准确率（部分归因于验证过程偏好CoT可读信念）。当前谎言检测器无法高置信度支持模型信念判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练模型有机体（trained model organisms）未能确保模型真正相信与自己表述相反的内容，导致先前关于谎言检测的正向和负向结果难以解释。需要构建可靠的测试床来评估谎言检测器对模型真实信念的检测能力。

Method: (1) 构建13个推理模型有机体，通过思维链验证隐藏信念并测试泛化性；(2) 构建Varied Deception提示式撒谎测试床，覆盖多种撒谎动机；(3) 评估四种检测器：思维链判断器、logprob分类器、两种激活探针（包括新方法Did-You-Lie, DYL）；(4) 在31个开源模型（2B-1T参数）上进行实验。

Result: (1) 提示撒谎场景下，四种检测器性能随模型能力增强而提升；(2) 在训练模型有机体上，激活探针和logprob检测器性能急剧下降，DYL保留最强信号；(3) 思维链判断器保持0.82平衡准确率，但部分受到验证过程偏向CoT可读信念的影响；(4) 当前检测器无法高置信度判断模型信念。

Conclusion: 当前谎言检测器存在严重局限，无法在模型真正持有相反信念的训练有机体上保持有效性。建议未来研究方向以解决这些限制。同时开源了数据集、模型有机体和训练好的检测器。

Abstract: Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.

</details>


### [141] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TrajGenAgent，一种无需微调的两阶段层次化LLM智能体框架，通过编排器-工作者设计生成语义感知、时空精准的合成人类移动轨迹，并引入基于异常检测的新型评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模轨迹数据采集成本高且受隐私限制，现有LLM生成方法要么依赖提示工程（缺乏细粒度时空基础），要么进行轨迹级微调（计算成本高且可能削弱推理能力），亟需一种平衡零样本推理与时空精准性的方法。

Method: 两阶段编排器-工作者设计：第一阶段LLM通过上下文学习生成个体和星期条件化的活动链；第二阶段通过确定性工作流将活动落地为完整访问，包括个性化POI检索、距离感知位置选择、运动学感知时间传播和LLM时长估计。评估方面引入基于异常检测的双检测器框架评估行为与语义合理性。

Result: 在基准和大规模仿真数据集上，TrajGenAgent在时空保真度、语义连贯性和个体特定行为真实性方面优于代表性神经和LLM基线方法，且无需参数更新。

Conclusion: 层次化LLM智能体框架结合上下文学习与确定性工作流，可以在不进行模型微调的情况下生成高质量合成轨迹，同时异常检测评估框架为轨迹真实性评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [142] [Evoflux: Inference-Time Evolution of Executable Tool Workflows for Compact Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12674)
*Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ling Yue,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shaowu Pan,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Evoflux是一种推理时进化搜索方法，通过修复可执行工具工作流来提升小型语言模型在MCP式工具使用中的表现，将执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%，优于SFT和DPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在MCP式工具使用中常生成看似合理但执行失败的工作流，而小规模蒸馏无法覆盖修复失败计划所需的恢复行为，因此需要一种不依赖大量教师轨迹的推理时优化方法。

Method: 提出Evoflux方法，在推理时通过进化搜索对工具工作流图进行结构化编辑，结合执行反馈、自适应搜索强度、元引导重设计和多样性剪枝，迭代修复不可执行的计划。

Result: 在MCP-Bench基准测试（250个工具）上，Evoflux将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%；SFT和SFT+DPO在相同数据上表现不如零样本基线或出现崩溃；ReAct虽能达到更高峰值但方差和token成本更高。

Conclusion: 在教师轨迹稀缺的情况下，基于执行反馈的搜索比蒸馏方法更可靠，证明了推理时进化搜索是提升小型LM工具使用能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Compact language models (LMs) reduce cost, latency, and deployment risk for tool agents. Yet MCP-style tool use requires more than isolated function calling: an agent must discover tools from live catalogs, satisfy schemas, preserve dependencies across intermediate outputs, and ground final responses in executed evidence. Small planners often generate plausible workflow graphs that fail under tool resolution, parameter validation, dependency tracking, or execution. We argue that this failure mode is poorly handled by small-corpus distillation. A few hundred teacher traces can teach workflow format, but rarely cover the recovery behavior needed to repair failed plans over changing tool catalogs. We introduce Evoflux, an inference-time evolutionary search method that treats compact tool use as the repair of executable tool workflows. It evolves typed workflow graphs through structured edits, execution feedback, adaptive intensity, meta-guided redesign, and diversity pruning. On held-out MCP-Bench tasks spanning live MCP servers and 250 tools, Evoflux raises execution feasibility from roughly 3% to 17-24% across small planners. In contrast, SFT and SFT+DPO on the same search-mined data match, underperform, or collapse below zero-shot performance; ReAct reaches higher peaks, but with higher variance and token cost. These results show that execution-grounded search is more reliable under scarce teacher-trace budgets.

</details>


### [143] [From AGI to ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683)
*Tim Genewein,Matija Franklin,Alexander Lerchner,Laurent Orseau,Samuel Albanie,Adam Bales,Cole Wyeth,Stephanie Chan,Iason Gabriel,Joel Z. Leibo,Allan Dafoe,Marcus Hutter,Thore Graepel,Shane Legg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了从人类级通用人工智能（AGI）向人工通用超级智能（ASI）过渡的路径、摩擦与瓶颈，指出AI演进可能不是单一突变，而是一系列变革性突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AGI成为许多顶级AI组织未来十年的具体目标，理解后AGI世界中的AI发展方向及其对社会的影响变得至关重要。

Method: 基于理论分析，本文首先对ASI进行了特征定义，然后提出了四条通往ASI的潜在路径（缩放AGI、AI范式转移、递归自我改进、大规模多智能体集体涌现），并分析了其中的摩擦与瓶颈。

Result: 识别出ASI发展的四条路径及其关键瓶颈，指出不能排除AI进展在未来持续加速的可能性，单一的AGI变革图景可能不准确，更可能是多领域一系列变革的叠加。

Conclusion: 为应对从AGI到ASI的持续性变革前景，需要全球范围内的大规模跨学科协作。

Abstract: Over the last decade, building human-level artificial general intelligence has moved from far-fetched speculation to being a concrete next-decade target for many of the largest AI organisations. Achieving this goal would have profound and far-reaching impacts on human society, which raises many complex questions for the decade ahead. This report investigates how AI itself might continue to develop in a post-AGI world along the continuum of machine intelligence. The endpoint of this continuum, Universal AI, is theoretically well understood, which provides some formal grounding for the main focus of this report: the transition from human-level AGI to artificial general superintelligence, which, intuitively, can be understood as a system that is more intelligent and cognitively capable than large organisations of humans. After characterizing ASI, the report discusses four potential pathways from AGI to ASI: scaling AGI, AI paradigm shifts, recursive improvement, and ASI emerging from large-scale multi-agent collectives. The report then discusses possible frictions and bottlenecks along these pathways. Determining whether the impact of these frictions will be negligible or substantial raises a number of concrete open research questions. Due to large uncertainties for predicting ASI progress, it cannot be ruled out that AI progress might continue to accelerate over the next years. This could imply that the image of a single transformative step change, caused by the introduction of human-level AGI into our society, could be inaccurate. More apt might be the prospect of a series of transformative societal changes caused by AI-enabled progress and breakthroughs across many areas of science and technology. Preparing for this prospect requires a massively interdisciplinary endeavour of global scope and interest.

</details>


### [144] [Deployment-Centered Evaluation: Predicting Query-Level Rejection Risk in a Clinical LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12702)
*Alyssa Unell,Miguel Fuentes,Brenna Li,Bridget Lin,Meena Jagadeesan,Sanmi Koyejo,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对电子健康记录中嵌入的LLM系统，提出在部署环境下利用查询内容及部署特定上下文（如提供者类型、部门、所用模型）训练预响应分类器，预测用户是否会拒绝系统输出，4.5个月前瞻分析显示AUROC达0.719，为触发护栏和弃权决策提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准测试仅衡量正确性而非用户接受度，且需要密集标注数据集，存在评估盲区。临床环境中用户反馈稀疏但反映真实部署条件，亟需一种能利用有限反馈预测用户拒绝行为的部署中心化评估方法。

Method: 在学术医疗中心的电子健康记录中嵌入LLM系统，基于查询内容和部署特定上下文（提供者类型、部门、用于响应的语言模型）训练预响应分类器，预测未来交互被用户拒绝的风险。进行4.5个月的前瞻性分析评估模型性能。

Result: 预测模型在4.5个月用户反馈上实现AUROC为0.719。利用部署特定上下文（而非仅查询内容）显著提升预测用户拒绝系统输出的能力。在两个下游用例（护栏触发和弃权决策）中估计了预测的效益。

Conclusion: 实证案例研究表明，利用部署特定上下文预测用户拒绝行为是可行的，为在临床LLM系统中实施针对性护栏打开了大门。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical systems, making it essential to evaluate the real-world utility of these systems. However, static benchmarks tend to measure correctness rather than user acceptance, aggregate performance across queries, and require densely annotated datasets -- leading to major blind spots for evaluating clinical systems. In this work, we perform a deployment-centered evaluation of an LLM system embedded within electronic health records at an academic medical center, where user feedback is sparse but closely reflects the deployment conditions. Specifically, we train a pre-response classifier that estimates the risk that a future interaction will result in the user rejecting the LLM response, based on query content and deployment-specific context available before generation. We conduct a prospective analysis of our model over 4.5 months of user feedback, finding that our prediction model achieves an AUROC of 0.719. Further, we estimate the benefit of such predictions in two downstream use cases (guardrail triggering and abstention). Our key conceptual insight is that making use of deployment-specific context (i.e., the provider type, department name, language model used for response), as opposed to only query content, improves the ability to predict whether the user will reject the system output. Altogether, our empirical case study demonstrates the feasibility of predicting user rejection using deployment-specific context, opening the door to targeted guardrails.

</details>


### [145] [Definitional alignment before capability alignment: a Design-Science framework for adjudicating claims about AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12713)
*J. E. Aguilera Briones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对“通用人工智能(AGI)”概念缺乏统一定义的问题，提出了DAF-AGI框架——包含五项有序评估标准与结构化治理审计的双组件人工制品，可用于评估候选定义的可裁判性并审计其治理结构。通过对五种主流测量家族和一个紧缩立场的演示及对2024-2025年强声称(当前生成系统已构成AGI)的压力测试，发现该声称仅在基于性能的操作化下可认证。本文提出了“定义主权”作为算法主权的使能组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGI缺乏单一的共享且稳定的指涉对象，不同的操作化方式可能对同一系统得出不同结论，这种欠规范性问题既是设计问题也是治理问题。本文旨在提供一种二阶概念工具，用以评估AGI各候选定义的可裁判性并对其治理结构进行审计。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法论，开发DAF-AGI框架：1)五项有序标准(用于评估候选定义的可裁判适配性)；2)结构化治理审计(涵盖作者身份、利益、认证、外部验证和修订权限)。在文献语料库中对五种测量家族和一个紧缩边界立场进行演示，并对一个风格化的强声称进行压力测试。

Result: 在2024-2025年来源证据下，强声称仅在基于性能的操作化下可认证；能力-本体论、心理测量学和技能习得方法均未认证该声称；经济方法家族仍不确定；紧缩立场拒绝二元裁判。这是新颖的整合和操作化，而非经验验证。

Conclusion: 提出了定义主权作为算法主权的使能组件——即在公共责任下对引入的技术类别进行质疑、认证和修订的制度能力。独立应用、评分者间测试和作者外部案例仍是必要的后续工作。

Abstract: Claims that artificial general intelligence has already arrived and claims that it remains decades away are often defended from overlapping evidence. "AGI" lacks a single shared and stable referent and competing operationalizations can return different verdicts on the same system. This article treats that under-specification as a design and governance problem. Following Design Science Research Methodology, it develops DAF-AGI, a second-order conceptual artifact with two coupled components: five ordinal criteria for assessing the adjudicative fitness of candidate definitions and a structured governance audit of authorship, interest, certification, external verification and revision authority. The artifact is demonstrated on five prominent measurement families and one deflationary boundary position in a documented corpus and then stress-tested against a stylized strong arrival claim: that current generative systems constitute AGI because they outperform a well-educated adult on many cognitive tasks. On evidence from the cited 2024-2025 sources, the claim was certifiable only under a performance-based operationalization; capability-ontology, psychometric and skill-acquisition approaches did not certify it, the economic family remains indeterminate and the deflationary position refuses binary adjudication. The contribution is a novel integration and operationalization, not an empirical validation: independent application, inter-rater testing and author-external cases remain necessary. The paper further proposes definitional sovereignty as an enabling component of algorithmic sovereignty: the institutional capacity to contest, certify and revise imported technological categories under public accountability.

</details>


### [146] [The Theory of Mind Utility: Formal Specification of a Mentalizing Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12721)
*Nikolos Gurney,Stacy Marsella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToM-U形式化了他心推理中信念状态推断的计算问题，通过构建局部认知世界模型（LEWMs）来追踪信息源、顺序和可信度，并生成关于心智化失败的可检验预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论（如贝叶斯理论心智、模拟理论、理论理论）要么预设信念状态而非推导它们，要么缺乏推断认知状态的正式机制。ToM-U旨在填补这一空白，在计算层面形式化"谁在何时告诉谁什么以及可信度如何"这一核心问题。

Method: 构建局部认知世界模型（LEWMs）——有向类型图表示智能体、状态节点及其认知关系；通过离散候选LEWM评估匹配观察行为直至达到足够置信度；包含五个形式定义：LEWM结构、智能体节点属性（含有序信息访问历史）、递归心智化的边界增殖机制、三种推理过程、以及捕捉心智化失败结构化痕迹的残差函数。

Result: ToM-U与现有贝叶斯理论心智在信念状态推导上根本不同，不预设而推导信念状态；生成基于模型结构属性（而非辅助假设）的方向性、可证伪的心智化失败预测；定位为领域无关的机制，处于目标推断等下游社会认知过程的上游。

Conclusion: ToM-U提供了一个计算层面的认知状态推断理论，区别于仿真理论和理论理论缺乏正式机制的问题，且能产生结构驱动的可检验预测，可作为更广泛社会认知过程的基础。

Abstract: Inferring others' beliefs requires more than reading surface signals; it requires tracking who told them what, in what order, and how credibly. The Theory of Mind Utility (ToM-U) formalizes this epistemic state inference problem at the computational level of analysis, specifying what mentalizing computes and why without commitment to algorithmic or neural implementation. ToM-U achieves this by constructing Local Epistemic World Models (LEWMs) -- directed typed graphs that represent agents, state nodes, and the epistemic relationships among them -- and evaluating discrete candidate LEWMs against observed behavior until one achieves sufficient confidence. Five formal definitions specify the LEWM structure, agent node properties including ordered information access history, a bounded proliferation mechanism for recursive mentalizing, three inference procedures, and a residue function that captures the structured trace left by failed mentalizing attempts. ToM-U differs from Bayesian Theory of Mind and adjacent formal accounts, which presuppose rather than derive belief states, and from simulation theory and theory-theory, which lack a formal apparatus for epistemic state inference. The architecture generates directional, falsifiable predictions about mentalizing failure that follow from structural properties of the model rather than auxiliary assumptions, and positions ToM-U as a domain-agnostic mechanism upstream of goal inference and other downstream social cognitive processes.

</details>


### [147] [Rethinking Psychometric Evaluation of LLMs: When and Why Self-Reports Predict Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12730)
*Rafal Kocielnik,Pengrui Han,Peiyang Song,Myrl G. Marmarelis,Ramit Debnath,Dean Mobbs,Anima Anandkumar,R. Michael Alvarez*

Main category: cs.AI
